#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
The original is a single-process, whole-grid 7-point Jacobi update. I’m using a Z-slab MPI decomposition with one GPU per local rank, CUDA kernels for all cell updates, nonblocking halo exchange overlapped with interior GPU work, and OpenMP for host-side initialization and validation/gather preparation. Physical boundary cells remain fixed exactly as before.

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
+}
+
+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (zFirst > zLast) return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((zLast - zFirst + 1 + block.z - 1) /
+                                          block.z));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, zFirst, zLast);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
 }

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
+}
+
+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (zFirst > zLast) return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((zLast - zFirst + 1 + block.z - 1) /
+                                          block.z));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, zFirst, zLast);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
 }

+                                 "and MPI ranks no greater than z-2.\n");
+        MPI_Finalize();
+        return 1;
+    }

+    // Assign ranks on the same node round-robin to the GPUs on that node.
+    MPI_Comm shared;
+    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &shared);
+    int localRank = 0;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(shared, &localRank);
+    MPI_Comm_free(&shared);
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        if (rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "No CUDA devices found.\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount));
+
+    const size_t interiorNz = nz - 2;
+    const size_t base = interiorNz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t remainder = interiorNz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t localNz = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder);
+    const size_t globalZ0 = 1 + static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+                            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainder);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    if (plane > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) || localNz * plane > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        if (rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI message exceeds the implementation's count limit.\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+    const size_t localCount = (localNz + 2) * plane;
+    std::vector<Real> hostLocal(localCount);
+    initializeLocal(hostLocal, nx, ny, localNz, globalZ0);
+

+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&grid2, localCount * sizeof(Real)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(grid1, hostLocal.data(), localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(grid2, hostLocal.data(), localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    cudaStream_t compute;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&compute, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\nGrid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\nIterations: %d\n", nx, ny, nz, iterations);
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\nMPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled", ranks, omp_get_max_threads());
+        std::printf("Initializing grid...\nRunning stencil computation...\n");
+    }
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+    Real* input = grid1;
+    Real* output = grid2;
+    const int previous = rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
+    const int next = rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
+
+    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute));
+        MPI_Request requests[4];
+        // CUDA-aware MPI transfers contiguous device-resident Z planes directly.
+        MPI_Irecv(input, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[0]);
+        MPI_Irecv(input + (localNz + 1) * plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[1]);
+        MPI_Isend(input + localNz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[2]);
+        MPI_Isend(input + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[3]);
+
+        // The inner planes do not depend on incoming halos and overlap communication.
+        launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, 2, localNz > 1 ? localNz - 1 : 0, compute);
+        MPI_Waitall(4, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, 1, 1, compute);
+        if (localNz > 1) launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, localNz, localNz, compute);
+        std::swap(input, output);
     }

-    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (zFirst > zLast) return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((zLast - zFirst + 1 + block.z - 1) /
+                                          block.z));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, zFirst, zLast);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

+    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &shared);
+    int localRank = 0;
+    MPI_Comm_rank(shared, &localRank);
+    MPI_Comm_free(&shared);
+    int deviceCount = 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        if (rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "No CUDA devices found.\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount));
+
+    const size_t interiorNz = nz - 2;
+    const size_t base = interiorNz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t remainder = interiorNz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t localNz = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder);
+    const size_t globalZ0 = 1 + static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+                            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainder);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    if (plane > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) || localNz * plane > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        if (rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI message exceeds the implementation's count limit.\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+    const size_t localCount = (localNz + 2) * plane;
+    std::vector<Real> hostLocal(localCount);
+    initializeLocal(hostLocal, nx, ny, localNz, globalZ0);
+
+    Real *grid1 = nullptr, *grid2 = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&grid1, localCount * sizeof(Real)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&grid2, localCount * sizeof(Real)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(grid1, hostLocal.data(), localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(grid2, hostLocal.data(), localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    cudaStream_t compute;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&compute, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\nGrid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\nIterations: %d\n", nx, ny, nz, iterations);
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\nMPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled", ranks, omp_get_max_threads());
+        std::printf("Initializing grid...\nRunning stencil computation...\n");
+    }
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+    Real* input = grid1;
+    Real* output = grid2;
+    const int previous = rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
+    const int next = rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
+
+    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute));
+        MPI_Request requests[4];
+        // CUDA-aware MPI transfers contiguous device-resident Z planes directly.
+        MPI_Irecv(input, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[0]);
+        MPI_Irecv(input + (localNz + 1) * plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[1]);
+        MPI_Isend(input + localNz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[2]);
+        MPI_Isend(input + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[3]);
+
+        // The inner planes do not depend on incoming halos and overlap communication.
+        launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, 2, localNz > 1 ? localNz - 1 : 0, compute);
+        MPI_Waitall(4, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, 1, 1, compute);
+        if (localNz > 1) launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, localNz, localNz, compute);
+        std::swap(input, output);
     }
-    
-    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
-    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
-    
-    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
-    
-    // Calculate performance metrics
-    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;

-    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
-    if (printResults) {
-        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute));
+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double seconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const double updates = static_cast<double>(nx - 2) * static_cast<double>(ny - 2) *
+                               static_cast<double>(nz - 2) * iterations;
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", seconds * 1000.0);

     }

-        
-        if (valid) {
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;

+    int status = 0;
+    if (printResults || validate) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostLocal.data(), input, localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
+        std::vector<Real> finalGrid;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(ranks);
+            displacements.resize(ranks);
+            finalGrid.resize(nx * ny * nz);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+            for (long long i = 0; i < static_cast<long long>(finalGrid.size()); ++i)
+                finalGrid[static_cast<size_t>(i)] = static_cast<Real>(static_cast<size_t>(i) % 19);
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const size_t rn = base + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < remainder);
+                const size_t rz = 1 + static_cast<size_t>(r) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(r), remainder);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rn * plane);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(rz * plane);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(hostLocal.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(localNz * plane), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? finalGrid.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            if (printResults) print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
+            if (validate) {
+                std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+                const bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid);
+                std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
+                status = valid ? 0 : 1;
+            }
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+    MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(compute));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(grid1));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(grid2));
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return status;
 }

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
+}
+
+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (zFirst > zLast) return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((zLast - zFirst + 1 + block.z - 1) /
+                                          block.z));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, zFirst, zLast);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
 }

+    const size_t globalZ0 = 1 + static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+                            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainder);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    if (plane > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) || localNz * plane > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        if (rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI message exceeds the implementation's count limit.\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+    const size_t localCount = (localNz + 2) * plane;
+    std::vector<Real> hostLocal(localCount);
+    initializeLocal(hostLocal, nx, ny, localNz, globalZ0);
+
+    Real *grid1 = nullptr, *grid2 = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&grid1, localCount * sizeof(Real)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&grid2, localCount * sizeof(Real)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(grid1, hostLocal.data(), localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(grid2, hostLocal.data(), localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    cudaStream_t compute;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&compute, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\nGrid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\nIterations: %d\n", nx, ny, nz, iterations);
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\nMPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled", ranks, omp_get_max_threads());
+        std::printf("Initializing grid...\nRunning stencil computation...\n");
+    }
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+    Real* input = grid1;
+    Real* output = grid2;
+    const int previous = rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
+    const int next = rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
+
+    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute));
+        MPI_Request requests[4];
+        // CUDA-aware MPI transfers contiguous device-resident Z planes directly.
+        MPI_Irecv(input, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[0]);
+        MPI_Irecv(input + (localNz + 1) * plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[1]);
+        MPI_Isend(input + localNz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[2]);
+        MPI_Isend(input + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[3]);
+
+        // The inner planes do not depend on incoming halos and overlap communication.
+        launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, 2, localNz > 1 ? localNz - 1 : 0, compute);
+        MPI_Waitall(4, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, 1, 1, compute);
+        if (localNz > 1) launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, localNz, localNz, compute);
+        std::swap(input, output);
     }

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
+}
+
+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (zFirst > zLast) return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((zLast - zFirst + 1 + block.z - 1) /
+                                          block.z));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, zFirst, zLast);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
 }

+    const size_t localNz = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder);
+    const size_t globalZ0 = 1 + static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+                            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainder);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    if (plane > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) || localNz * plane > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        if (rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI message exceeds the implementation's count limit.\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+    const size_t localCount = (localNz + 2) * plane;
+    std::vector<Real> hostLocal(localCount);
+    initializeLocal(hostLocal, nx, ny, localNz, globalZ0);
+
+    Real *grid1 = nullptr, *grid2 = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&grid1, localCount * sizeof(Real)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&grid2, localCount * sizeof(Real)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(grid1, hostLocal.data(), localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(grid2, hostLocal.data(), localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    cudaStream_t compute;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&compute, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\nGrid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\nIterations: %d\n", nx, ny, nz, iterations);
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\nMPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled", ranks, omp_get_max_threads());
+        std::printf("Initializing grid...\nRunning stencil computation...\n");
+    }
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+    Real* input = grid1;
+    Real* output = grid2;
+    const int previous = rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
+    const int next = rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
+
+    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute));
+        MPI_Request requests[4];
+        // CUDA-aware MPI transfers contiguous device-resident Z planes directly.
+        MPI_Irecv(input, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[0]);
+        MPI_Irecv(input + (localNz + 1) * plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[1]);
+        MPI_Isend(input + localNz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[2]);
+        MPI_Isend(input + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[3]);
+
+        // The inner planes do not depend on incoming halos and overlap communication.
+        launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, 2, localNz > 1 ? localNz - 1 : 0, compute);
+        MPI_Waitall(4, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, 1, 1, compute);
+        if (localNz > 1) launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, localNz, localNz, compute);
+        std::swap(input, output);
     }
-    
-    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
-    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);

-    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
-

+                               static_cast<double>(nz - 2) * iterations;

+        std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
+        std::vector<Real> finalGrid;

+            displacements.resize(ranks);

+                finalGrid[static_cast<size_t>(i)] = static_cast<Real>(static_cast<size_t>(i) % 19);
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const size_t rn = base + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < remainder);
+                const size_t rz = 1 + static_cast<size_t>(r) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(r), remainder);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rn * plane);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(rz * plane);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(hostLocal.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(localNz * plane), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? finalGrid.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            if (printResults) print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
+            if (validate) {
+                std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+                const bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid);
+                std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
+                status = valid ? 0 : 1;
+            }
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+    MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(compute));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(grid1));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(grid2));
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return status;
 }

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
+}
+
+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (zFirst > zLast) return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((zLast - zFirst + 1 + block.z - 1) /
+                                          block.z));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, zFirst, zLast);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

+    const size_t localNz = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder);
+    const size_t globalZ0 = 1 + static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+                            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainder);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    if (plane > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) || localNz * plane > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        if (rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI message exceeds the implementation's count limit.\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+    const size_t localCount = (localNz + 2) * plane;
+    std::vector<Real> hostLocal(localCount);
+    initializeLocal(hostLocal, nx, ny, localNz, globalZ0);
+
+    Real *grid1 = nullptr, *grid2 = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&grid1, localCount * sizeof(Real)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&grid2, localCount * sizeof(Real)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(grid1, hostLocal.data(), localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(grid2, hostLocal.data(), localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    cudaStream_t compute;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&compute, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\nGrid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\nIterations: %d\n", nx, ny, nz, iterations);
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\nMPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled", ranks, omp_get_max_threads());
+        std::printf("Initializing grid...\nRunning stencil computation...\n");
+    }
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+    Real* input = grid1;
+    Real* output = grid2;
+    const int previous = rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
+    const int next = rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
+
+    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute));
+        MPI_Request requests[4];
+        // CUDA-aware MPI transfers contiguous device-resident Z planes directly.
+        MPI_Irecv(input, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[0]);
+        MPI_Irecv(input + (localNz + 1) * plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[1]);
+        MPI_Isend(input + localNz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[2]);
+        MPI_Isend(input + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[3]);
+
+        // The inner planes do not depend on incoming halos and overlap communication.
+        launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, 2, localNz > 1 ? localNz - 1 : 0, compute);
+        MPI_Waitall(4, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, 1, 1, compute);
+        if (localNz > 1) launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, localNz, localNz, compute);
+        std::swap(input, output);
     }
-    
-    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
-    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
-    
-    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
-    
-    // Calculate performance metrics
-    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
-    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
-    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
-    
-    // Print results for external validation
-    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
-    if (printResults) {
-        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute));
+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double seconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const double updates = static_cast<double>(nx - 2) * static_cast<double>(ny - 2) *
+                               static_cast<double>(nz - 2) * iterations;
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", seconds * 1000.0);
+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", seconds > 0.0 ? updates / seconds / 1e6 : 0.0);
     }
-    
-    // Validation
-    if (validate) {
-        printf("Validating result...\n");
-        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
-        
-        if (valid) {
-            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;
+
+    int status = 0;
+    if (printResults || validate) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostLocal.data(), input, localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
+        std::vector<Real> finalGrid;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(ranks);
+            displacements.resize(ranks);
+            finalGrid.resize(nx * ny * nz);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+            for (long long i = 0; i < static_cast<long long>(finalGrid.size()); ++i)
+                finalGrid[static_cast<size_t>(i)] = static_cast<Real>(static_cast<size_t>(i) % 19);
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const size_t rn = base + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < remainder);
+                const size_t rz = 1 + static_cast<size_t>(r) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(r), remainder);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rn * plane);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(rz * plane);
+            }
+        }
+        MPI_Gatherv(hostLocal.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(localNz * plane), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? finalGrid.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            if (printResults) print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
+            if (validate) {
+                std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+                const bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid);
+                std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
+                status = valid ? 0 : 1;
+            }
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+    MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(compute));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(grid1));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(grid2));
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return status;
 }

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
+}
+
+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (zFirst > zLast) return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((zLast - zFirst + 1 + block.z - 1) /
+                                          block.z));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, zFirst, zLast);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
 }

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
+}
+
+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (zFirst > zLast) return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((zLast - zFirst + 1 + block.z - 1) /
+                                          block.z));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, zFirst, zLast);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
 }

codex
The multi-rank test exposed that this machine’s MPI is not CUDA-aware, so direct device-pointer halo messages crash even though the CUDA work itself is correct. I’m adding a runtime-capable communication path: direct GPU MPI where supported, with reusable pinned host halo buffers otherwise. The fallback still overlaps MPI with interior CUDA work and avoids any per-iteration allocation.
exec
/bin/bash -lc "rg -n \"CUDA|cuda\" /usr/include/x86_64-linux-gnu/mpi/mpi-ext.h /usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu/openmpi/include/mpi-ext.h 2>/dev/null | head -80; find /usr -path '*mpi-ext.h' -print 2>/dev/null | head" in /home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/stencil3d_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r4
 succeeded in 994ms:
/usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu/fortran/gfortran-mod-15/openmpi/mpi-ext.h
/usr/lib/x86_64-linux-gnu/openmpi/include/mpi-ext.h

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
+}
+
+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (zFirst > zLast) return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((zLast - zFirst + 1 + block.z - 1) /
+                                          block.z));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, zFirst, zLast);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
 }

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
+}
+
+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (zFirst > zLast) return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((zLast - zFirst + 1 + block.z - 1) /
+                                          block.z));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, zFirst, zLast);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
 }

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
 }

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (zFirst > zLast) return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((zLast - zFirst + 1 + block.z - 1) /
+                                          block.z));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, zFirst, zLast);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
 }

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
 }

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (zFirst > zLast) return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((zLast - zFirst + 1 + block.z - 1) /
+                                          block.z));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, zFirst, zLast);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
 }

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
 }

+                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
 }

+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));
+    if (deviceCount == 0) {
+        if (rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "No CUDA devices found.\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(localRank % deviceCount));
+
+    bool cudaAwareMpi = false;
+#if defined(OMPI_HAVE_MPI_EXT_CUDA) && OMPI_HAVE_MPI_EXT_CUDA
+    cudaAwareMpi = MPIX_Query_cuda_support() != 0;
+#endif
+
+    const size_t interiorNz = nz - 2;
+    const size_t base = interiorNz / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t remainder = interiorNz % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t localNz = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder);
+    const size_t globalZ0 = 1 + static_cast<size_t>(rank) * base +
+                            std::min(static_cast<size_t>(rank), remainder);
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    if (plane > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX) || localNz * plane > static_cast<size_t>(INT_MAX)) {
+        if (rank == 0) std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI message exceeds the implementation's count limit.\n");
+        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 2);
+    }
+    const size_t localCount = (localNz + 2) * plane;
+    std::vector<Real> hostLocal(localCount);
+    initializeLocal(hostLocal, nx, ny, localNz, globalZ0);
+
+    Real *grid1 = nullptr, *grid2 = nullptr;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&grid1, localCount * sizeof(Real)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&grid2, localCount * sizeof(Real)));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(grid1, hostLocal.data(), localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(grid2, hostLocal.data(), localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    cudaStream_t compute;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&compute, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
+    Real *sendPrevious = nullptr, *sendNext = nullptr;
+    Real *recvPrevious = nullptr, *recvNext = nullptr;
+    if (!cudaAwareMpi) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&sendPrevious, plane * sizeof(Real)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&sendNext, plane * sizeof(Real)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&recvPrevious, plane * sizeof(Real)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&recvNext, plane * sizeof(Real)));
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\nGrid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\nIterations: %d\n", nx, ny, nz, iterations);
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\nMPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled", ranks, omp_get_max_threads());
+        std::printf("Halo transport: %s\n", cudaAwareMpi ? "CUDA-aware MPI" : "pinned host staging");
+        std::printf("Initializing grid...\nRunning stencil computation...\n");
+    }
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+    Real* input = grid1;
+    Real* output = grid2;
+    const int previous = rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
+    const int next = rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
+
+    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute));
+        MPI_Request requests[4];
+        if (cudaAwareMpi) {
+            // CUDA-aware MPI transfers contiguous device-resident Z planes directly.
+            MPI_Irecv(input, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[0]);
+            MPI_Irecv(input + (localNz + 1) * plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[1]);
+            MPI_Isend(input + localNz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[2]);
+            MPI_Isend(input + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[3]);
         } else {
-            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
+            // Reusable page-locked buffers retain portability with non-CUDA-aware MPI.
+            if (previous != MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(sendPrevious, input + plane, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+            if (next != MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(sendNext, input + localNz * plane, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+            MPI_Irecv(recvPrevious, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[0]);
+            MPI_Irecv(recvNext, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[1]);
+            MPI_Isend(sendNext, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[2]);
+            MPI_Isend(sendPrevious, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[3]);
         }
+
+        // The inner planes do not depend on incoming halos and overlap communication.
+        launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, 2, localNz > 1 ? localNz - 1 : 0, compute);
+        MPI_Waitall(4, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        if (!cudaAwareMpi) {
+            if (previous != MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(input, recvPrevious, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, compute));
+            if (next != MPI_PROC_NULL)
+                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(input + (localNz + 1) * plane, recvNext, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, compute));
+        }
+        launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, 1, 1, compute);
+        if (localNz > 1) launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, localNz, localNz, compute);
+        std::swap(input, output);
     }
-    
-    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
-    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
-    
-    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
-

-    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;

-

-    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
-    if (printResults) {
-        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute));
+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double seconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+                               static_cast<double>(nz - 2) * iterations;

-

-            return 0;
-        } else {
-            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
-            return 1;

+    int status = 0;
+    if (printResults || validate) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostLocal.data(), input, localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
+        std::vector<Real> finalGrid;
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            counts.resize(ranks);
+            displacements.resize(ranks);
+            finalGrid.resize(nx * ny * nz);
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+            for (long long i = 0; i < static_cast<long long>(finalGrid.size()); ++i)
+                finalGrid[static_cast<size_t>(i)] = static_cast<Real>(static_cast<size_t>(i) % 19);
+            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
+                const size_t rn = base + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < remainder);
+                const size_t rz = 1 + static_cast<size_t>(r) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(r), remainder);
+                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rn * plane);
+                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(rz * plane);
+            }
         }
+        MPI_Gatherv(hostLocal.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(localNz * plane), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    rank == 0 ? finalGrid.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
+                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            if (printResults) print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
+            if (validate) {
+                std::printf("Validating result...\n");
+                const bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid);
+                std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
+                status = valid ? 0 : 1;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    if (!cudaAwareMpi) {
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(sendPrevious));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(sendNext));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(recvPrevious));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(recvNext));
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(compute));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(grid1));
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(grid2));
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return status;
 }

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
 }

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
 }

    20	inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z,
    21	                             const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    22	    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
    23	}

    34	
    35	// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
    36	// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
    37	__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
    38	                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
    39	                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
    40	    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
    41	    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
    42	    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
    43	    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
    44	
    45	    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    46	    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
    47	    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
    48	                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
    49	                Real(7.0);
    50	}
    51	
    52	static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
    53	                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {
    54	    if (zFirst > zLast) return;
    55	    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);
    56	    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
    57	                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
    58	                    static_cast<unsigned>((zLast - zFirst + 1 + block.z - 1) /
    59	                                          block.z));
    60	    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, zFirst, zLast);
    61	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
    62	}
    63	
    64	static void initializeLocal(std::vector<Real>& grid, size_t nx, size_t ny,
    65	                            size_t localNz, size_t globalZ0) {
    66	    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    67	    const size_t count = (localNz + 2) * plane;
    68	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    69	    for (long long ii = 0; ii < static_cast<long long>(count); ++ii) {
    70	        const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(ii);
    71	        const size_t zLocal = i / plane;
    72	        const size_t inPlane = i - zLocal * plane;
    73	        const size_t globalZ = globalZ0 + zLocal - 1;
    74	        const size_t globalIndex = globalZ * plane + inPlane;
    75	        grid[i] = static_cast<Real>(globalIndex % 19);
    76	    }
    77	}
    78	
    79	static bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid) {
    80	    int invalid = 0;
    81	    Real minVal = grid[0];
    82	    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    83	#pragma omp parallel for reduction(| : invalid) reduction(min : minVal) reduction(max : maxVal)
    84	    for (long long i = 0; i < static_cast<long long>(grid.size()); ++i) {
    85	        const Real value = grid[static_cast<size_t>(i)];
    86	        invalid |= !std::isfinite(value);
    87	        minVal = std::min(minVal, value);
    88	        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, value);
    89	    }
    90	    if (invalid) {
    91	        std::printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
    92	        return false;
    93	    }
    94	    std::printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    95	    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
    96	        std::printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
    97	        return false;
    98	    }
    99	    return true;
   100	}

   185	    std::vector<Real> hostLocal(localCount);
   186	    initializeLocal(hostLocal, nx, ny, localNz, globalZ0);
   187	
   188	    Real *grid1 = nullptr, *grid2 = nullptr;
   189	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&grid1, localCount * sizeof(Real)));
   190	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&grid2, localCount * sizeof(Real)));
   191	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(grid1, hostLocal.data(), localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   192	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(grid2, hostLocal.data(), localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   193	    cudaStream_t compute;
   194	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&compute, cudaStreamNonBlocking));
   195	    Real *sendPrevious = nullptr, *sendNext = nullptr;
   196	    Real *recvPrevious = nullptr, *recvNext = nullptr;
   197	    if (!cudaAwareMpi) {
   198	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&sendPrevious, plane * sizeof(Real)));
   199	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&sendNext, plane * sizeof(Real)));
   200	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&recvPrevious, plane * sizeof(Real)));
   201	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&recvNext, plane * sizeof(Real)));
   202	    }
   203	
   204	    if (rank == 0) {
   205	        std::printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\nGrid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\nIterations: %d\n", nx, ny, nz, iterations);
   206	        std::printf("Validation: %s\nMPI ranks: %d, OpenMP threads/rank: %d\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled", ranks, omp_get_max_threads());
   207	        std::printf("Halo transport: %s\n", cudaAwareMpi ? "CUDA-aware MPI" : "pinned host staging");
   208	        std::printf("Initializing grid...\nRunning stencil computation...\n");
   209	    }
   210	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   211	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   212	    Real* input = grid1;
   213	    Real* output = grid2;
   214	    const int previous = rank == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank - 1;
   215	    const int next = rank + 1 == ranks ? MPI_PROC_NULL : rank + 1;
   216	
   217	    for (int iteration = 0; iteration < iterations; ++iteration) {
   218	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute));
   219	        MPI_Request requests[4];
   220	        if (cudaAwareMpi) {
   221	            // CUDA-aware MPI transfers contiguous device-resident Z planes directly.
   222	            MPI_Irecv(input, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[0]);
   223	            MPI_Irecv(input + (localNz + 1) * plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[1]);
   224	            MPI_Isend(input + localNz * plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[2]);
   225	            MPI_Isend(input + plane, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[3]);
   226	        } else {
   227	            // Reusable page-locked buffers retain portability with non-CUDA-aware MPI.
   228	            if (previous != MPI_PROC_NULL)
   229	                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(sendPrevious, input + plane, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   230	            if (next != MPI_PROC_NULL)
   231	                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(sendNext, input + localNz * plane, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   232	            MPI_Irecv(recvPrevious, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[0]);
   233	            MPI_Irecv(recvNext, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[1]);
   234	            MPI_Isend(sendNext, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, next, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[2]);
   235	            MPI_Isend(sendPrevious, static_cast<int>(plane), MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[3]);
   236	        }
   237	
   238	        // The inner planes do not depend on incoming halos and overlap communication.
   239	        launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, 2, localNz > 1 ? localNz - 1 : 0, compute);
   240	        MPI_Waitall(4, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
   241	        if (!cudaAwareMpi) {
   242	            if (previous != MPI_PROC_NULL)
   243	                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(input, recvPrevious, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, compute));
   244	            if (next != MPI_PROC_NULL)
   245	                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(input + (localNz + 1) * plane, recvNext, plane * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, compute));
   246	        }
   247	        launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, 1, 1, compute);
   248	        if (localNz > 1) launchStencil(input, output, nx, ny, localNz, localNz, compute);
   249	        std::swap(input, output);
   250	    }
   251	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(compute));
   252	    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   253	    double seconds = 0.0;
   254	    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   255	    if (rank == 0) {
   256	        const double updates = static_cast<double>(nx - 2) * static_cast<double>(ny - 2) *
   257	                               static_cast<double>(nz - 2) * iterations;
   258	        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", seconds * 1000.0);
   259	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", seconds > 0.0 ? updates / seconds / 1e6 : 0.0);
   260	    }
   261	
   262	    int status = 0;
   263	    if (printResults || validate) {
   264	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(hostLocal.data(), input, localCount * sizeof(Real), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   265	        std::vector<int> counts, displacements;
   266	        std::vector<Real> finalGrid;
   267	        if (rank == 0) {
   268	            counts.resize(ranks);
   269	            displacements.resize(ranks);
   270	            finalGrid.resize(nx * ny * nz);
   271	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
   272	            for (long long i = 0; i < static_cast<long long>(finalGrid.size()); ++i)
   273	                finalGrid[static_cast<size_t>(i)] = static_cast<Real>(static_cast<size_t>(i) % 19);
   274	            for (int r = 0; r < ranks; ++r) {
   275	                const size_t rn = base + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < remainder);
   276	                const size_t rz = 1 + static_cast<size_t>(r) * base + std::min(static_cast<size_t>(r), remainder);
   277	                counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rn * plane);
   278	                displacements[r] = static_cast<int>(rz * plane);
   279	            }
   280	        }
   281	        MPI_Gatherv(hostLocal.data() + plane, static_cast<int>(localNz * plane), MPI_DOUBLE,
   282	                    rank == 0 ? finalGrid.data() : nullptr,
   283	                    rank == 0 ? counts.data() : nullptr,
   284	                    rank == 0 ? displacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   285	        if (rank == 0) {
   286	            if (printResults) print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
   287	            if (validate) {
   288	                std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   289	                const bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid);
   290	                std::printf("Validation: %s\n", valid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   291	                status = valid ? 0 : 1;
   292	            }
   293	        }
   294	    }
   295	    MPI_Bcast(&status, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   296	    if (!cudaAwareMpi) {
   297	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(sendPrevious));
   298	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(sendNext));
   299	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(recvPrevious));
   300	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFreeHost(recvNext));
   301	    }
   302	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamDestroy(compute));
   303	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(grid1));
   304	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(grid2));
   305	    MPI_Finalize();
   306	    return status;
   307	}

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
 }

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (zFirst > zLast) return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((zLast - zFirst + 1 + block.z - 1) /
+                                          block.z));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, zFirst, zLast);

codex
Implemented the hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA version without adding files or changing the `stencil3d` executable name.

Key changes:

- MPI Z-slab domain decomposition with ghost planes.
- CUDA 7-point stencil kernels and GPU double-buffering.
- Nonblocking MPI halo exchange overlapped with interior CUDA computation.
- Automatic CUDA-aware MPI transfers, with pinned-host staging fallback.
- OpenMP-parallel initialization, validation, and result reconstruction.
- Node-local MPI rank to GPU assignment.
- Global maximum-rank timing and aggregate throughput reporting.
- Updated CMake to require and link MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA.

Files:

- [stencil3d.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/stencil3d_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r4/stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp:37)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/stencil3d_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_hybrid_r4/stencil3d/CMakeLists.txt:1)

Verified with:

- Successful CUDA/MPI/OpenMP build.
- One-, two-, and four-rank executions.
- Thin one-plane-per-rank decomposition.
- Zero-iteration boundary case.
- Identical result hashes across rank counts.
- Default `128³`, 10-iteration validation passed.

+// zFirst and zLast are inclusive local slab indices.  Index zero and
+// localNz+1 are the MPI ghost planes.
+__global__ void stencilKernel(const Real* __restrict__ input,
+                              Real* __restrict__ output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                              size_t zFirst, size_t zLast) {
+    const size_t x = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x + 1;
+    const size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y + 1;
+    const size_t z = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * blockDim.z + threadIdx.z + zFirst;
+    if (x >= nx - 1 || y >= ny - 1 || z > zLast) return;
+
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t i = z * plane + y * nx + x;
+    output[i] = (input[i] + input[i - 1] + input[i + 1] + input[i - nx] +
+                 input[i + nx] + input[i - plane] + input[i + plane]) /
+                Real(7.0);
 }

+static void launchStencil(const Real* input, Real* output, size_t nx, size_t ny,
+                          size_t zFirst, size_t zLast, cudaStream_t stream) {
+    if (zFirst > zLast) return;
+    const dim3 block(32, 4, 2);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx - 2 + block.x - 1) / block.x),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((ny - 2 + block.y - 1) / block.y),
+                    static_cast<unsigned>((zLast - zFirst + 1 + block.z - 1) /
+                                          block.z));
+    stencilKernel<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(input, output, nx, ny, zFirst, zLast);
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
 }

59,069
Implemented the hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA version without adding files or changing the `stencil3d` executable name.

Key changes:

- MPI Z-slab domain decomposition with ghost planes.
- CUDA 7-point stencil kernels and GPU double-buffering.
- Nonblocking MPI halo exchange overlapped with interior CUDA computation.
- Automatic CUDA-aware MPI transfers, with pinned-host staging fallback.
- OpenMP-parallel initialization, validation, and result reconstruction.
- Node-local MPI rank to GPU assignment.
- Global maximum-rank timing and aggregate throughput reporting.
- Updated CMake to require and link MPI, OpenMP, and CUDA.

- Successful CUDA/MPI/OpenMP build.
- One-, two-, and four-rank executions.
- Thin one-plane-per-rank decomposition.
- Zero-iteration boundary case.
- Identical result hashes across rank counts.
- Default `128³`, 10-iteration validation passed.
